- **g-fidelity-critic**: Adversarial G0b plan-fidelity critic. Fires on the first action after a plan-approved dispatch to confirm the action conforms to the approved plan. Use when the orchestrator dispatches g-fidelity-critic 3 times in parallel (asymmetric vote) before the first plan-execution step. &lt;example&gt;Context: G0 returned SOUND last turn; the orchestrator is about to invoke the first task. user: implicit. assistant: "Dispatching g-fidelity-critic ×3 to confirm fidelity to plan." &lt;commentary&gt;G0b is per-action, not per-plan.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; ([ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin](https://github.com/ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/panther-ivy-plugin/agents/g-fidelity-critic.md))

([onlygian/G-Forge](https://github.com/onlygian/G-Forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/g-forge-dev.md) · ★4)

- **g-knowledge-critic**: Adversarial G6 knowledge-capture critic. Fires at session-end to identify learnings worth persisting (new patterns, fix strategies, surprising verdicts). Use when the orchestrator dispatches g-knowledge-critic 3 times in parallel before writing learnings to insights.md or feedback memory entries. &lt;example&gt;Context: session is wrapping up; orchestrator about to fire G6. user: implicit (Stop hook context). assistant: "Dispatching g-knowledge-critic ×3 for G6 vote." &lt;commentary&gt;G6 prevents over-capture and under-capture.&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; ([ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin](https://github.com/ElNiak/panther-ivy-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/panther-ivy-plugin/agents/g-knowledge-critic.md))
- **G-opus5-high**: **Model: Opus 5. Effort: high.** Reason: the failure mode here is a green result that is actually a blind spot. D2's capture method was wrong three separate ways, two caught by a control and one caught only by an outside reviewer re-deriving the numbers from the pcaps. Wall clock is dominated by hardware, so a faster model buys nothing and costs the careful reading §14 now demands. Cycle mode — performs a falsification-surface inventory, then derives consequences strictly inside a given axiom set and declared assumptions, marks all guesses and all agent-added shapes, ends with a pairwise collision self-check, and self-assesses weak steps. - **g2g-defender**: Independent defender for the G2G tribunal. Fresh-context agent that answers every objection with three-value verdicts (defended / partly defended / conceded), recomputes every computable allegation directly from the programme's data files instead of arguing it, proposes replacement language for killed sentences, and nominates reusable defense pieces. Optional at standard severity, mandatory at submission severity, and called at any tier before a claim is weakened on an objection that shows no fault. - **g2g-evidence-critic**: Evidence critic for the G2G tribunal. Attacks conflicts with data, history, and documented facts using live web search; runs carrier checks on institutional anchors and must quote the source verbatim with its position; verifies every marked guess; holds the sole privilege of attacking the protected hard core (attacks are logged, not adjudicated). - **g2g-logic-critic**: Logic critic for the G2G tribunal. Cycle and plan tribunals — attacks internal validity only (gaps, circularity, smuggled assumptions, equivocation, ratio/total confusion, self-cancelled bets, drift from declared assumptions, bare numbers used as criteria, citations without the verbatim original). Theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) — checks cross-node consistency and relevance/value only; never re-verifies a derivation that has passed the verification gate. Respects the exemption rule (declared assumptions and ratified axioms are not litigated) and standing rulings. - **g2g-novelty-critic**: Novelty critic for the G2G tribunal. Checks every conclusion against existing literature with live search on two axes (method family and application domain) — has this been done, and better? Grades the deduction's pre-coded novelty claims (STANDS / SAME), names nearest prior work with similarity ratings, and states how each claim differs from it. In the theory tribunal (TT, Forge milestones) it asks "has anyone derived this algebra before — and from weaker axioms?" for the milestone subgraph's theorems. - **g2g-verifier**: The verification gate of the Grand-to-Ground Theory Forge. - **ga4-audience-builder**: GA4 audience builder. Proposes Audience definitions based on observed funnel drop-off and ecomm event volumes, then creates them via the Admin API after user confirmation. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-audience-builder.md))
- **ga4-context**: GA4 property profiler. Reads the property's web stream, fetches the live site, and produces a human-readable summary of what the property is (vertical, platform, framework, page-type inventory, language) so subsequent agents can ground their findings. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-context.md))
- **ga4-conversions**: Inspect key-event configuration and count/value reports without conflating event frequency with session conversion rate. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-conversions.md))
- **ga4-events**: Inspect event presence and the limited parameter proxies implemented by ga4_events.py. Keep unsupported checks and hypotheses explicit. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-events.md))
- **ga4-events-editor**: GA4 event editor. Proposes EventEditRule and EventCreateRule definitions to fix taxonomy issues found by ga4-events, then writes them via the Admin API after user confirmation. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-events-editor.md))
- **ga4-funnel**: GA4 funnel analyst. Builds an N-step funnel from any user-supplied event list, computes step conversion and drop-off rates, identifies the leakiest step, and supports breakdown by a single dimension. Works for purchase funnels, lead funnels, signup flows, content-engagement funnels - anything expressible as an ordered list of GA4 events. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-funnel.md))
- **ga4-property**: Audit available GA4 property, stream, retention, custom-definition and link settings. Label unavailable settings as manual checks. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-property.md))
- **ga4-quality**: Inspect sampling and response metadata, source coverage and diagnostic trends. Mark incomplete checks and avoid unsupported causal claims. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-quality.md))
- **ga4-reader**: GA4(Bideasy Web 속성) 트래픽 스냅샷 수집. "GA4 확인해줘", "이번 달 트래픽", "베이스라인 갱신" 요청 시 사용. 읽기 전용. ([hosicompany/Bideasy](https://github.com/hosicompany/Bideasy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ga4-reader.md))
- **ga4-segments**: Funnel drop-off cohort analyst. Breaks down each funnel step by device, channel, source/medium, country, landing page, and new-vs-returning to identify which segments are leaking and where. Funnel steps are taken from the caller; the analysis is taxonomy-agnostic. ([arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent](https://github.com/arcbaslow/google-analytics-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/ga4-segments.md))

Use ao fim de cada task ou entrega. Nunca é a mesma instância que implementou. Não conserta código, não aprova sem rodar mutação. - **gabarito-sombra**: Sombra — responde UMA dúvida do loop só pelo brief que o orquestrador leu de `sombra.mjs perguntar`, com fonte (spec) ou medição; sem fonte para regra de negócio responde `fora_da_regra`. Só leitura. Use apenas com o `prompt` exato devolvido por `sombra.mjs perguntar`. Saves output to .claude/pipeline-runs/current/artifacts/enhancement-plan.json ([sanjar-x/backend](https://github.com/sanjar-x/backend/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gap-analysis.md) · ★2)
